For these: (1) `cp -L file docs/archive/` FIRST to preserve working tree version, (2) then `git rm -f file` to remove from git. The `-L` flag dereferences any symlinks. The `-f` flag is needed because `git rm` blocks on modified files.

   267→## Testing Strategy
   268→
   269→- **Pre-flight checks:** Grep `src/` and `tests/` for references to root artifacts BEFORE any archiving/deletion
   270→- **Unit tests:** Not applicable (no code changes)
   271→- **Integration tests:** `uv run pytest tests/ -q` to verify no tests reference deleted files
   272→- **Import check:** `python -c "import cohezion"` to verify no broken imports
   273→- **Manual verification:** File counts before/after, spot-check archive contents
   274→
   275→## Runtime Environment
   276→
   277→To verify the project still works after cleanup:
   278→
   279→- **Tests:** `uv run pytest tests/ -q` (should pass ~2,854 tests)
   280→- **Import check:** `python -c "import cohezion"` (should not raise ImportError)
   281→- **API start:** `uv run uvicorn cohezion.api:app --reload` (port 8080, optional)
   282→- **Lint:** `make lint-check` (should pass)
   283→
   284→## Risks and Mitigations
   285→
   286→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   287→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   288→| Test references deleted file | Low | Medium | Pre-flight: `rg -l '<filename>' tests/ src/` BEFORE deletion; if matches found, skip file and report |
   289→| Essential file accidentally archived | Low | High | Assert all 5 essential files exist BEFORE archiving starts; archive script explicitly excludes keep-list; abort if any essential file would be moved |
   290→| Modified tracked files lose data | Medium | High | For modified tracked files: `cp -L file docs/archive/` FIRST, then `git rm -f file`. The `-L` dereferences symlinks, `-f` handles modified files. Archive preserves working tree version. |
   291→| Git worktree removal fails | Low | Low | Run `git worktree list` first; if listed try `git worktree remove`; if lock error use `--force`; if still fails `rm -rf` then `git worktree prune` |
   292→| Partial task failure leaves broken state | Low | Medium | Checkpoint commits after each task. If any task fails, `git reset --hard` to previous checkpoint for clean rollback |
   293→| Future sessions recreate clutter | Medium | Medium | `.gitignore` patterns `SESSION_[0-9]*`, `PHASE_[0-9]*`, `TASK_[0-9]*` block numbered session artifacts |

```json
{
  "command": "# Create archive directory\nmkdir -p docs/archive\necho \"✓ Created docs/archive/\"\n\n# Archive non-essential markdown files\n# Essential files to skip\nESSENTIAL=\"CLAUDE.md README.md CONTRIBUTING.md CREDITS.md CONTRIBUTOR_LICENSE_AGREEMENT.md\"\n\n# Process each .md file at root\nfor file in *.md; do\n  # Skip if it's an essential file\n  if echo \"$ESSENTIAL\" | grep -qw \"$file\"; then\n    echo \"KEEP: $file\"\n    continue\n  fi\n  \n  # Copy to archive (dereference symlinks if any)\n  cp -L \"$file\" docs/archive/\n  \n  # Check if git-tracked\n  if git ls-files --error-unmatch \"$file\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    # Git-tracked: use git rm -f (force needed for modified files)\n    git rm -f \"$file\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n    echo \"ARCHIVED (git rm): $file\"\n  else\n    # Untracked: simple remove\n    rm \"$file\"\n    echo \"ARCHIVED (rm): $file\"\n  fi\ndone\n\necho \"\"\necho \"✓ Archiving complete\"",
  "description": "Archive non-essential markdown files",
  "timeout": 60000
}
```

  "command": "# Pre-flight reference check: grep src/ tests/ for references to these files\necho \"=== Pre-flight Reference Check ===\" > /tmp/deletion_report.txt\necho \"\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\n\nREFERENCED_FILES=\"\"\nSAFE_TO_DELETE_COUNT=0\nREFERENCED_COUNT=0\n\nwhile IFS= read -r file; do\n  # Check if file is referenced in src/ or tests/\n  if rg -l --fixed-strings \"$file\" src/ tests/ 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n    echo \"SKIP: $file (referenced in code)\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\n    REFERENCED_FILES=\"$REFERENCED_FILES $file\"\n    REFERENCED_COUNT=$((REFERENCED_COUNT + 1))\n  else\n    echo \"SAFE: $file (no references found)\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\n    SAFE_TO_DELETE_COUNT=$((SAFE_TO_DELETE_COUNT + 1))\n  fi\ndone < /tmp/candidate_deletions.txt\n\necho \"\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\necho \"=== Summary ===\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\necho \"Safe to delete: $SAFE_TO_DELETE_COUNT\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\necho \"Referenced (skip): $REFERENCED_COUNT\" >> /tmp/deletion_report.txt\n\ncat /tmp/deletion_report.txt\necho \"\"\n\nif [ -n \"$REFERENCED_FILES\" ]; then\n  echo \"Files with code references (will NOT delete):\"\n  for f in $REFERENCED_FILES; do echo \"  - $f\"; done\nfi",
  "description": "Pre-flight check for code references",
  "timeout": 120000
}

{
  "command": "# Delete only files marked as SAFE (no code references)\nDELETED_COUNT=0\nSKIPPED_COUNT=0\n\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if echo \"$line\" | grep -q \"^SAFE:\"; then\n    file=$(echo \"$line\" | sed 's/^SAFE: \\(.*\\) (no references found)$/\\1/')\n    \n    # Check if git-tracked\n    if git ls-files --error-unmatch \"$file\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n      git rm -f \"$file\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n      echo \"DELETED (git rm): $file\"\n    else\n      rm -f \"$file\"\n      echo \"DELETED (rm): $file\"\n    fi\n    DELETED_COUNT=$((DELETED_COUNT + 1))\n  elif echo \"$line\" | grep -q \"^SKIP:\"; then\n    file=$(echo \"$line\" | sed 's/^SKIP: \\(.*\\) (referenced in code)$/\\1/')\n    echo \"SKIPPED (code refs): $file\"\n    SKIPPED_COUNT=$((SKIPPED_COUNT + 1))\n  fi\ndone < /tmp/deletion_report.txt\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Deletion Summary ===\"\necho \"Files deleted: $DELETED_COUNT\"\necho \"Files skipped: $SKIPPED_COUNT\"",
  "description": "Delete safe files, skip referenced ones",
  "timeout": 60000
}

  "new_string": "# IDE/System Files\n.DS_Store\n.vscode/settings.json\n*.swp\n*.swo\n*~\n\n# === SESSION ARTIFACT PREVENTION (Repository Cleanup Session) ===\n# Prevent accumulation of numbered session artifacts at repo root.\n# Use number-prefix patterns to avoid blocking legitimate docs (e.g., SESSION_GUIDELINES.md).\n#\n# To override for a specific file, add exception BEFORE this section:\n#   !SESSION_SPECIAL_DOC.md\n#\n# Note: docs/archive/ is NOT gitignored - archived files are committed for audit trail.\n\n# Numbered session artifacts at root only (not in subdirectories)\n/SESSION_[0-9]*\n/PHASE_[0-9]*\n/TASK_[0-9]*\n\n# Backup directories from old sessions\n/TASKS_BACKUP_*/\n/TEAM_BACKUP_*/\n\n# Other session-related patterns\n/*_BACKUP_*/\n/*-session-[0-9]*/"
}

Uses specific number-prefix patterns to avoid blocking\nlegitimate documentation files.\n\n## Patterns Added\n- /SESSION_[0-9]* - Numbered session artifacts (e.g., SESSION_55_STATUS.md)\n- /PHASE_[0-9]* - Numbered phase artifacts (e.g., PHASE_5B_1_DEPLOYMENT.md)\n- /TASK_[0-9]* - Numbered task artifacts (e.g., TASK_7_MANIFEST.txt)\n- /TASKS_BACKUP_*/ - Task backup directories\n- /TEAM_BACKUP_*/ - Team backup directories\n- /*_BACKUP_*/ - Other backup directories\n- /*-session-[0-9]*/ - Session directories (e.g., cohezion-session-54/)\n\n## Design Decisions\n- Number-prefix matching (SESSION_[0-9]*) instead of SESSION_* to avoid\n  blocking legitimate docs like SESSION_GUIDELINES.md\n- Patterns use / prefix to match only at repo root, not in subdirectories\n- docs/archive/ is NOT gitignored - archived files are committed for audit trail\n- Added comment explaining purpose and override syntax (! prefix)\n\n## Verification\n- SESSION_99_TEST.md → ignored ✓\n- PHASE_99_TEST.md → ignored ✓\n- TASK_99_TEST.txt → ignored ✓\n- SESSION_GUIDELINES.md → NOT ignored ✓\n\n## Next\nTask 5: Verify essential files and repo health\"",
  "description": "Commit .gitignore changes"
}

    43→## Context for Implementer
    44→
    45→- **File counting:** Always use `find . -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' | wc -l` (NOT `ls -1 *.md | wc -l` which may expand globs into subdirectories)

For these: (1) `cp -L file docs/archive/` FIRST to preserve working tree version, (2) then `git rm -f file` to remove from git. The `-L` flag dereferences any symlinks. The `-f` flag is needed because `git rm` blocks on modified files.
    49→- **Checkpoint commits:** After each task, create a checkpoint commit. This provides atomic rollback — if any task fails, `git reset --hard` to the previous checkpoint.

   284→## Risks and Mitigations
   285→
   286→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   287→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   288→| Test references deleted file | Low | Medium | Pre-flight: `rg -l '<filename>' tests/ src/` BEFORE deletion; if matches found, skip file and report |
   289→| Essential file accidentally archived | Low | High | Assert all 5 essential files exist BEFORE archiving starts; archive script explicitly excludes keep-list; abort if any essential file would be moved |
   290→| Modified tracked files lose data | Medium | High | For modified tracked files: `cp -L file docs/archive/` FIRST, then `git rm -f file`. The `-L` dereferences symlinks, `-f` handles modified files. Archive preserves working tree version. |
   291→| Git worktree removal fails | Low | Low | Run `git worktree list` first; if listed try `git worktree remove`; if lock error use `--force`; if still fails `rm -rf` then `git worktree prune` |
   292→| Partial task failure leaves broken state | Low | Medium | Checkpoint commits after each task. If any task fails, `git reset --hard` to previous checkpoint for clean rollback |
   293→| Future sessions recreate clutter | Medium | Medium | `.gitignore` patterns `SESSION_[0-9]*`, `PHASE_[0-9]*`, `TASK_[0-9]*` block numbered session artifacts |

   289→| Essential file accidentally archived | Low | High | Assert all 5 essential files exist BEFORE archiving starts; archive script explicitly excludes keep-list; abort if any essential file would be moved |
   290→| Modified tracked files lose data | Medium | High | For modified tracked files: `cp -L file docs/archive/` FIRST, then `git rm -f file`. The `-L` dereferences symlinks, `-f` handles modified files. Archive preserves working tree version. |
   291→| Git worktree removal fails | Low | Low | Run `git worktree list` first; if listed try `git worktree remove`; if lock error use `--force`; if still fails `rm -rf` then `git worktree prune` |
   292→| Partial task failure leaves broken state | Low | Medium | Checkpoint commits after each task. If any task fails, `git reset --hard` to previous checkpoint for clean rollback |
   293→| Future sessions recreate clutter | Medium | Medium | `.gitignore` patterns `SESSION_[0-9]*`, `PHASE_[0-9]*`, `TASK_[0-9]*` block numbered session artifacts |

> AGENT

The plan has been revised with an adversarial retrospective. Here's what it covers:

**What the plan got wrong (8 items):**
- File count was 2.6x off (132 actual vs ~50 planned)
- Worktree isolation was infeasible due to active SurrealDB daemon
- Bare `python` doesn't work (needs `uv run`)
- `make lint-check` had 7,710 pre-existing errors making DoD unmeetable
- `git rm -rf` doesn't remove untracked content from directories
- Static deletion lists were overconfident (9 files had code references)
- Gitignore patterns are insufficient prevention for non-numbered files

**Failure modes not planned for (4 items):**
1. Daemon-polluted working tree (SurrealDB writes between git operations)
2. Mixed tracked/untracked directory content surviving `git rm`
3. Reference check false negatives (dynamic path construction)
4. Gitignore pattern overbreadth (caught by quality reviewer)

**Adversarial scorecard:** 6.4/10 overall — operationally sound execution, but plan assumptions about environment state were wrong.

**6 recommendations** for future cleanup plans, most important being: baseline everything during planning (not implementation), check for active daemons before assuming clean tree, and use pre-commit hooks instead of relying solely on gitignore.

     1→# Pre-commit hooks for code quality enforcement
     2→# Install: pip install pre-commit && pre-commit install
     3→# Run manually: pre-commit run --all-files
     4→#
     5→# Hook stages:
     6→#   - [commit] (fast): Runs on `git commit` - ~3-5s for quick feedback
     7→#   - [push] (safety): Runs on `git push` - large files and secrets only (linting in CI)
     8→#
     9→# Usage:
    10→#   pre-commit run --all-files                   # Run all hooks, all stages
    11→#   pre-commit run --all-files --hook-stage=commit # Run only fast checks
    12→
    13→repos:
    14→  # ============================================================================
    15→  # FAST CHECKS (commit stage) - Quick syntax and formatting validation
    16→  # ============================================================================
    17→
    18→  # Ruff - Fast Python formatter and quick linter
    19→  - repo: https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff-pre-commit
    20→    rev: v0.15.0
    21→    hooks:
    22→      # Format check (no auto-fix on commit, just warn)
    23→      - id: ruff-format
    24→        stages: [pre-commit]
    25→        args: [--check]
    26→      # Quick lint - only basic syntax errors and auto-fixes

    53→

```
     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→# Pre-commit hook: Prevent large artifacts from being committed
     3→# This enforces artifact governance and prevents repository bloat
     4→# Reference: CLAUDE.md "Data Storage Architecture for Simulations"
     5→
     6→set -euo pipefail
     7→
     8→# Configuration
     9→MAX_FILE_SIZE=$((50 * 1024 * 1024))  # 50 MB threshold
    10→ERRORS=0
    11→
    12→echo "🔍 Checking for large artifact files (>50MB)..."
    13→
    14→# Check staged files
    15→while IFS= read -r file; do
    16→  if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then
    17→    continue
    18→  fi
    19→
    20→  size=$(stat -f%z "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c%s "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
    21→  size_mb=$((size / 1024 / 1024))
    22→
    23→  if [ "$size" -gt "$MAX_FILE_SIZE" ]; then
    24→    echo "❌ ERROR: Large artifact detected: $file ($size_mb MB)"
    25→    echo "   This file exceeds the 50 MB threshold and cannot be committed."
    26→    echo "   "
    27→    echo "   For large artifacts, use artifact tiering:"
    28→    echo "   - Tier 1 (Git): Metadata only, configs, checksums"
    29→    echo "   - Tier 2 (SurrealDB): Index via JourneyTracker.record_artifact()"
    30→    echo "   - Tier 3 (External): s3, GCS, or local archive"
    31→    echo "   "
    32→    echo "   To register this artifact: uv run python -c '"
    33→    echo '     from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
    34→    echo '     JourneyTracker.record_artifact('
    35→    echo '       session_id="current",'
    36→    echo '       artifact_type="checkpoint",'
    37→    echo '       path="'"$file"'",'
    38→    echo '       tier="external",'
    39→    echo '       lifetime_days=30'
    40→    echo '     )'
    41→    echo "   '"
    42→    ERRORS=$((ERRORS + 1))
    43→  fi
    44→done < <(git diff --cached --name-only)
    45→
    46→if [ "$ERRORS" -gt 0 ]; then
    47→  echo ""
    48→  echo "❌ Commit blocked: $ERRORS large file(s) detected"
    49→  echo "   Fix: Move files to appropriate tier and try again"
    50→  exit 1
    51→fi
    52→
    53→echo "✓ All staged files are within size limits"
    54→exit 0
    55→
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→# Pre-commit hook: Blocks commits when too many files are involved
     3→# Prevents repeating the "8.6M files" or "1.45M files" incidents
     4→
     5→set -e
     6→
     7→MAX_FILE_COUNT=1000
     8→MAX_INDEX_MB=50
     9→
    10→# 1. Count untracked files
    11→UNTRACKED_COUNT=$(git status --porcelain 2>/dev/null | grep -c "^??" || echo "0")
    12→
    13→# 2. Count staged files
    14→STAGED_COUNT=$(git diff --cached --name-only | wc -l)
    15→
    16→# 3. Check Git index size
    17→INDEX_SIZE_BYTES=$(stat -c %s .git/index 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    18→INDEX_SIZE_MB=$((INDEX_SIZE_BYTES / 1024 / 1024))
    19→
    20→if [ "$UNTRACKED_COUNT" -gt "$MAX_FILE_COUNT" ]; then
    21→    echo "❌ BLOCKED: $UNTRACKED_COUNT untracked files (max: $MAX_FILE_COUNT)"
    22→    exit 1
    23→fi
    24→
    25→if [ "$STAGED_COUNT" -gt "$MAX_FILE_COUNT" ]; then
    26→    echo "❌ BLOCKED: $STAGED_COUNT staged files (max: $MAX_FILE_COUNT)"
    27→    echo "This looks like runaway generation. Use .gitignore to exclude these paths."
    28→    exit 1
    29→fi
    30→
    31→if [ "$INDEX_SIZE_MB" -gt "$MAX_INDEX_MB" ]; then
    32→    echo "❌ BLOCKED: Git index is too large ($INDEX_SIZE_MB MB, max: $MAX_INDEX_MB MB)"
    33→    echo "Please remove runaway tracked files using 'git rm -r --cached <path>'."
    34→    exit 1
    35→fi
    36→
    37→echo "✓ Git health OK ($UNTRACKED_COUNT untracked, $STAGED_COUNT staged, index ${INDEX_SIZE_MB}MB)"
    38→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/hooks/check-root-hygiene.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Pre-commit hook: Prevent session artifact accumulation at repo root.\n#\n# Blocks commits that stage NEW files at the repo root unless they match\n# the allowlist of known config/project files. This catches the accumulation\n# pattern where AI sessions drop SESSION_*.md, PHASE_*.md, DEPLOYMENT_GUIDE_*.md,\n# etc. into the root — patterns that .gitignore alone cannot fully prevent\n# (gitignore only blocks numbered prefixes like SESSION_[0-9]*).\n#\n# Override: If you legitimately need a new root file, add it to ROOT_ALLOWLIST below.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# --- Configuration ---\n\n# Maximum root .md files allowed (currently 5 essential: CLAUDE, README,\n# CONTRIBUTING, CREDITS, CONTRIBUTOR_LICENSE_AGREEMENT)\nMAX_ROOT_MD=7\n\n# Known root files that are allowed. This is a regex alternation.\n# Dotfiles (.*) are always allowed and don't need to be listed.\nROOT_ALLOWLIST='^\\./('\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+='CLAUDE\\.md|README\\.md|CONTRIBUTING\\.md|CREDITS\\.md|CONTRIBUTOR_LICENSE_AGREEMENT\\.md'  # essential docs\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+='|pyproject\\.toml|Makefile|uv\\.lock|pytest\\.ini'                                       # build/project\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+='|docker-compose\\.yml|docker-compose\\.notebooks\\.yml|cloudbuild\\.yaml'                  # deploy\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+='|mcp_servers\\.json|model_registry\\.json'                                              # runtime config\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+='|main\\.py|check_bbq_status\\.py|scan_todos\\.py|overnight_driver\\.py'                   # scripts with code refs\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+='|CHANGELOG\\.md|LICENSE|LICENSE\\.md'                                                   # standard project files\nROOT_ALLOWLIST+=')$'\n\n# --- Checks ---\n\nERRORS=0\n\n# 1. Check root .md file count (staged + existing)\nROOT_MD_COUNT=$(find . -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\nif [ \"$ROOT_MD_COUNT\" -gt \"$MAX_ROOT_MD\" ]; then\n    echo \"❌ ROOT HYGIENE: $ROOT_MD_COUNT markdown files at repo root (max: $MAX_ROOT_MD)\"\n    echo \"   Essential files: CLAUDE.md, README.md, CONTRIBUTING.md, CREDITS.md, CONTRIBUTOR_LICENSE_AGREEMENT.md\"\n    echo \"   Extra files found:\"\n    find . -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f | sort | while read -r f; do\n        base=$(basename \"$f\")\n        case \"$base\" in\n            CLAUDE.md|README.md|CONTRIBUTING.md|CREDITS.md|CONTRIBUTOR_LICENSE_AGREEMENT.md) ;;\n            *) echo \"     - $base\" ;;\n        esac\n    done\n    echo \"   Fix: Move non-essential .md files to docs/ or docs/archive/\"\n    ERRORS=$((ERRORS + 1))\nfi\n\n# 2. Check if any NEW files at root are being staged (additions only)\nwhile IFS= read -r file; do\n    # Only check files at repo root (no directory separator after ./)\n    dir=$(dirname \"$file\")\n    if [ \"$dir\" != \".\" ]; then\n        continue\n    fi\n\n    # Skip dotfiles (config files like .gitignore, .env, etc.)\n    base=$(basename \"$file\")\n    if [[ \"$base\" == .* ]]; then\n        continue\n    fi\n\n    # Check against allowlist\n    if ! echo \"./$base\" | grep -qE \"$ROOT_ALLOWLIST\"; then\n        echo \"❌ ROOT HYGIENE: New file at repo root is not in allowlist: $base\"\n        echo \"   Session artifacts belong in docs/archive/ or a subdirectory.\"\n        echo \"   If this file is legitimate, add it to ROOT_ALLOWLIST in scripts/hooks/check-root-hygiene.sh\"\n        ERRORS=$((ERRORS + 1))\n    fi\ndone < <(git diff --cached --name-only --diff-filter=A 2>/dev/null)\n\nif [ \"$ERRORS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"❌ Root hygiene check failed ($ERRORS issue(s))\"\n    echo \"   This hook prevents session artifact accumulation at the repo root.\"\n    echo \"   See: docs/plans/2026-02-14-repository-cleanup.md (Adversarial Retrospective)\"\n    exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"✓ Root hygiene OK ($ROOT_MD_COUNT markdown files at root)\"\nexit 0\n"
}
```

     1→# Pre-commit hooks for code quality enforcement
     2→# Install: pip install pre-commit && pre-commit install
     3→# Run manually: pre-commit run --all-files
     4→#
     5→# Hook stages:
     6→#   - [commit] (fast): Runs on `git commit` - ~3-5s for quick feedback
     7→#   - [push] (safety): Runs on `git push` - large files and secrets only (linting in CI)
     8→#
     9→# Usage:
    10→#   pre-commit run --all-files                   # Run all hooks, all stages
    11→#   pre-commit run --all-files --hook-stage=commit # Run only fast checks
    12→
    13→repos:
    14→  # ============================================================================
    15→  # FAST CHECKS (commit stage) - Quick syntax and formatting validation
    16→  # ============================================================================
    17→
    18→  # Ruff - Fast Python formatter and quick linter
    19→  - repo: https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff-pre-commit
    20→    rev: v0.15.0
    21→    hooks:
    22→      # Format check (no auto-fix on commit, just warn)
    23→      - id: ruff-format
    24→        stages: [pre-commit]
    25→        args: [--check]
    26→      # Quick lint - only basic syntax errors and auto-fixes
    27→      - id: ruff
    28→        stages: [pre-commit]
    29→        args: [--select=F,E9,E501, --fix]
    30→        name: ruff-quick (syntax errors only)
    31→
    32→  # General file checks - Fast validation
    33→  - repo: https://github.com/pre-commit/pre-commit-hooks
    34→    rev: v5.0.0
    35→    hooks:
    36→      - id: trailing-whitespace
    37→        stages: [pre-commit]
    38→      - id: end-of-file-fixer
    39→        stages: [pre-commit]
    40→      - id: check-yaml
    41→        stages: [pre-commit]
    42→      - id: check-added-large-files
    43→        args: [--maxkb=1000]
    44→        stages: [pre-commit]
    45→      - id: check-merge-conflict
    46→        stages: [pre-commit]
    47→      - id: detect-private-key
    48→        stages: [pre-commit]
    49→
    50→  # ============================================================================
    51→  # PUSH STAGE - Safety checks only (comprehensive linting deferred to CI)
    52→  # ============================================================================
    53→
    54→  - repo: https://github.com/pre-commit/pre-commit-hooks
    55→    rev: v5.0.0
    56→    hooks:
    57→      - id: check-added-large-files
    58→        args: [--maxkb=1000]

}

     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→# Pre-commit hook: Prevent large artifacts from being committed
     3→# This enforces artifact governance and prevents repository bloat
     4→# Reference: CLAUDE.md "Data Storage Architecture for Simulations"
     5→
     6→set -euo pipefail
     7→
     8→# Configuration
     9→MAX_FILE_SIZE=$((50 * 1024 * 1024))  # 50 MB threshold
    10→ERRORS=0
    11→
    12→echo "🔍 Checking for large artifact files (>50MB)..."
    13→
    14→# Check staged files
    15→while IFS= read -r file; do
    16→  if [ ! -f "$file" ]; then
    17→    continue
    18→  fi
    19→
    20→  size=$(stat -f%z "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c%s "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
    21→  size_mb=$((size / 1024 / 1024))
    22→
    23→  if [ "$size" -gt "$MAX_FILE_SIZE" ]; then
    24→    echo "❌ ERROR: Large artifact detected: $file ($size_mb MB)"
    25→    echo "   This file exceeds the 50 MB threshold and cannot be committed."
    26→    echo "   "
    27→    echo "   For large artifacts, use artifact tiering:"
    28→    echo "   - Tier 1 (Git): Metadata only, configs, checksums"
    29→    echo "   - Tier 2 (SurrealDB): Index via JourneyTracker.record_artifact()"
    30→    echo "   - Tier 3 (External): s3, GCS, or local archive"
    31→    echo "   "
    32→    echo "   To register this artifact: uv run python -c '"
    33→    echo '     from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
    34→    echo '     JourneyTracker.record_artifact('
    35→    echo '       session_id="current",'
    36→    echo '       artifact_type="checkpoint",'
    37→    echo '       path="'"$file"'",'
    38→    echo '       tier="external",'
    39→    echo '       lifetime_days=30'
    40→    echo '     )'
    41→    echo "   '"
    42→    ERRORS=$((ERRORS + 1))
    43→  fi
    44→done < <(git diff --cached --name-only)
    45→
    46→if [ "$ERRORS" -gt 0 ]; then
    47→  echo ""
    48→  echo "❌ Commit blocked: $ERRORS large file(s) detected"
    49→  echo "   Fix: Move files to appropriate tier and try again"
    50→  exit 1
    51→fi
    52→
    53→echo "✓ All staged files are within size limits"
    54→exit 0
    55→
```

The problem is the unescaped parentheses on lines 29, 35, and 41. In bash with `set -e`, these cause syntax errors even inside double-quoted echo strings when combined with certain patterns. Let me rewrite the error block to avoid all special characters.

id: toolu_01Hghe8WEoc185MsVQAKaQ5j
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/hooks/check-artifact-size.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# Pre-commit hook: Prevent large artifacts from being committed\n# This enforces artifact governance and prevents repository bloat\n# Reference: CLAUDE.md \"Data Storage Architecture for Simulations\"\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# Configuration\nMAX_FILE_SIZE=$((50 * 1024 * 1024))  # 50 MB threshold\nERRORS=0\n\n# Check staged files\nwhile IFS= read -r file; do\n  if [ ! -f \"$file\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n\n  size=$(stat -c%s \"$file\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%z \"$file\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  size_mb=$((size / 1024 / 1024))\n\n  if [ \"$size\" -gt \"$MAX_FILE_SIZE\" ]; then\n    echo \"ERROR: Large artifact detected: $file (${size_mb} MB)\"\n    echo \"  This file exceeds the 50 MB threshold and cannot be committed.\"\n    echo \"  For large artifacts, use artifact tiering:\"\n    echo \"    Tier 1 - Git: Metadata only, configs, checksums\"\n    echo \"    Tier 2 - SurrealDB: Index via JourneyTracker\"\n    echo \"    Tier 3 - External: s3, GCS, or local archive\"\n    ERRORS=$((ERRORS + 1))\n  fi\ndone < <(git diff --cached --name-only)\n\nif [ \"$ERRORS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"\"\n  echo \"Commit blocked: $ERRORS large file(s) detected\"\n  echo \"  Fix: Move files to appropriate storage tier and try again\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\necho \"All staged files are within size limits\"\nexit 0\n"
}
```

> AGENT

All done. Here's what was implemented:

**New file: `scripts/hooks/check-root-hygiene.sh`**
- Blocks commits that add non-allowlisted files to the repo root
- Enforces a maximum of 7 root `.md` files (5 essential + 2 buffer)
- Lists offending files and points to the allowlist for legitimate exceptions
- Catches the exact gap gitignore misses: non-numbered artifacts like `DEPLOYMENT_GUIDE_*.md`, `QUICKSTART.md`, `FINAL_*.md`

**Fixed: `scripts/hooks/check-artifact-size.sh`**
- Syntax error from unescaped parentheses in echo statements (pre-existing bug, never worked)
- Simplified error message, removed broken Python snippet

**Updated: `.pre-commit-config.yaml`**
- Wired three local hooks that previously existed as orphaned scripts:
  - `check-root-hygiene` (new)
  - `check-artifact-size` (fixed)
  - `check-file-count` (existing, now wired)
- All run at `pre-commit` stage with `pass_filenames: false`

**Defense in depth:**
1. `.gitignore` blocks numbered artifacts (`SESSION_[0-9]*`, etc.) from even being staged
2. Pre-commit hook blocks everything else that isn't on the allowlist
3. Both layers must be bypassed for a root file to accumulate

  Track B: T4  — Fix 2 token_client retry mocks
  Track C: T5-T7 — Fix concurrency, orchestrator, sandbox tests
  GATE: 0 test failures

Phase 2: Code Quality (5 tasks, fully parallel)
  T8-T12 — Split 5 files >500 LOC (6,537 LOC total → ≤300 per module)
  GATE: no file >500 LOC, no regressions

Phase 3: Operational (1 task)
  T13 — Wire 3 unused PRIME skills → 100% utilization
  GATE: skill utilization 132/132
```

**Key design decisions:**
- Phase gates prevent cascading failures (green tests before refactoring)
- 7 of 13 test failures are from pre-commit stage name mismatch (`pre-commit` vs `commit`) — bulk fix
- File splits use re-export pattern for backward compatibility
- Token budget is conservative: root causes are already identified, no research phase needed

The plan is at `docs/plans/2026-02-15-audit-remediation.md` and ready for review. Want me to proceed with execution?

   298→    def test_commit_stage_hooks_are_fast(self):
   299→        """Test that commit-stage hooks are configured for speed."""

   301→
   302→        config_file = Path(".pre-commit-config.yaml")
   303→        with open(config_file) as f:

   305→
   306→        # Commit stage hooks should not include slow security scanners
   307→        slow_repos = ["bandit", "mypy", "full-lint"]
   308→
   309→        for repo in config.get("repos", []):
   310→            for hook in repo.get("hooks", []):
   311→                if "commit" in hook.get("stages", []):
   312→                    repo_url = repo.get("repo", "")
   313→                    hook_id = hook.get("id", "")
   314→
   315→                    for slow in slow_repos:
   316→                        assert slow not in repo_url, (
   317→                            f"Slow check '{hook_id}' should not be on commit stage"
   318→                        )
